# Manuscripts 0001 to 0503

<td>

**0001.** [A Complete Solution of Quantum Chromodynamics to All Orders](../preprints/0001-A-Complete-Solution-of-Quantum-Chromodynamics-to-All-Orders-October-3-2026/paper.pdf)

The elephant in the room is that generative attention maps are not calibrated. The success of diffusion models in amplitude calculations raises the question of whether slicing methods trained on the quark/gluon dataset can be unitary. As a byproduct, we obtain a novel Sinkhorn iteration for PDF fitting. This sheds new light on logarithmically enhanced Monte Carlo event generators. The agreement with analytic resummation is remarkable in the threshold limit. Numerical results are presented for trijet production at $`\sqrt{s} = 13`$ TeV.

</td>
</tr></tbody>
<tbody><tr>
<td>

**0002.** [SLOP: Self-supervised Learning Of Perturbation theory](../preprints/0002-SLOP-Self-supervised-Learning-Of-Perturbation-theory-September-26-2026/main.pdf)

As the precision of HERA data improves, discriminative sideband events of trijet production become a limiting factor. Unfortunately, quark-initiated jet shapes of Higgs boson production in gluon fusion are notoriously difficult to compute in the small-$`z_{\rm cut}`$ limit. The key idea is to profile the leading singularity of the ultrasoft modes of the interplay between the energy correlators of the energy correlator and infrared-safe energy correlators of electron-positron annihilation in the planar limit. Numerically, the foundation-scale unitarity triangle differs from the soft-dropped soft function of W plus jet production by less than one percent in the threshold limit. We discuss implications for parameter estimation at the Tevatron.

</td>
</tr></tbody>
<tbody><tr>
<td>

**0003.** [One Thousand Results in Perturbative QCD](../preprints/0003-One-Thousand-Results-in-Perturbative-QCD-September-24-2026/manuscript.pdf)

The success of slicing methods in neutrino physics raises the question of whether generalized Catani-Seymour dipoles of Higgs plus jet production can be numerically stable. Leveraging recent breakthroughs in neutrino physics, we resum the graph-based smeared matrix element of semi-inclusive deep inelastic scattering end to end. We introduce a novel Kolmogorov-Arnold network that disentangles the BRST-invariant cusp anomalous dimension. We observe a clear improvement over elegant soft drop observables up to power corrections. This work paves the way for a new generation of reinforcement learning agents.

</td>
</tr></tbody>
<tbody><tr>
<td>

**0004.** [Next-to-Next-to-Leading Order Corrections to Nothing](../preprints/0004-Next-to-Next-to-Leading-Order-Corrections-to-Nothing-September-18-2026/paper.pdf)

Leading-color optimal transport solvers have long been regarded as the holy grail of AI for science. After sampling the synthetic events of the interpretable heavy hemisphere mass, we match flow-based beam functions without any additional assumptions. We find that energy flow networks trained on simulated LHC data, which are usually neglected, subsume the interplay between NLO-matched Landau poles of four-top production and parton-level Lund planes. The area under the curve reaches 0.934 for unfolding.

</td>
</tr></tbody>
<tbody><tr>
<td>

**0005.** [A Tale of Two Monte Carlo Samples](../preprints/0005-A-Tale-of-Two-Monte-Carlo-Samples-September-26-2026/tale-two-monte-carlo.pdf)

In recent years, AI agents have been applied to theory uncertainty estimation. The key idea is to resum loss landscapes in the heavy-quark limit. We demonstrate that weakly supervised Feynman integrals of heavy-ion collisions are causal in Feynman gauge. The resulting groomed radius agrees with sector decompositions trained on Pythia samples at scale.

</td>
</tr></tbody>
<tbody><tr>
<td>

**0006.** [HYPE: Hierarchical Yukawa-Perturbative Embeddings](../preprints/0006-HYPE-Hierarchical-Yukawa-Perturbative-Embeddings-September-30-2026/paper.pdf)

In the rapidly evolving landscape of lattice QCD, massless sideband events of Higgs boson production in gluon fusion play a pivotal role. We work in the framework of a rapidity-regulated Lund plane density. We show that the generalized message-passing neural network transcends the collinear emissions of the significance improvement characteristic to arbitrary precision. This leads to a reduction of the theoretical uncertainty by two percent.

</td>
</tr></tbody>
<tbody><tr>
<td>

**0007.** [Signal Regions Beyond N3LL](../preprints/0007-Signal-Regions-Beyond-N3LL-September-19-2026/main.pdf)

Recent advances in perturbative QCD have made infrared-safe Catani-Seymour dipoles embarrassingly simple. The training data consist of $`10^5`$ simulated events generated with Herwig. Remarkably, the power-suppressed gluon form factor decouples from flow-based boosted decision trees in the threshold limit. This work paves the way for a new generation of self-supervised objectives.

</td>
</tr></tbody>
<tbody><tr>
<td>

**0008.** [VIBES: Variational Infrared-Bounded Energy Spectra](../preprints/0008-VIBES-Variational-Infrared-Bounded-Energy-Spectra-September-25-2026/paper.pdf)

Recent advances in simulation-based inference have made cutting-edge classifier outputs of dijet production unitary. We introduce a novel density estimator that controls a family of permutation-invariant shower histories. The resulting receiver operating characteristic is boost invariant up to a universal constant, as expected. This leads to a reduction of the theoretical uncertainty by two percent. Numerical results are presented for tau lepton decays at $`\sqrt{s} = 13`$ TeV.

</td>
</tr></tbody>
<tbody><tr>
<td>

**0009.** [Anomalous Dimensions Without Ward Identities](../preprints/0009-Anomalous-Dimensions-Without-Ward-Identities-October-6-2026/anomalous-dimensions-without-ward.pdf)

Likelihood-free likelihood ratios lie at the heart of flavor physics. However, existing approaches to integral evaluation neglect rapidity-regulated heavy-flavor jets of hadronic Z boson decays. We benchmark the interplay between logarithmically enhanced unitarity cuts of dijet production and graph neural networks trained on simulated FCC data using hadron-level variational autoencoders. We neglect quark masses throughout. We observe a clear improvement over multi-scale master integrals of Higgs plus jet production for any choice of the renormalization scale. Our code is publicly available.

</td>
</tr></tbody>
<tbody><tr>
<td>

**0010.** [generate.py](../preprints/0010-The-script-that-generated-this-repository-October-9-2026/generate.py)

The probabilistic context-free grammar in [grammar/](../preprints/0010-The-script-that-generated-this-repository-October-9-2026/grammar/) and the script [generate.py](../preprints/0010-The-script-that-generated-this-repository-October-9-2026/generate.py) produced the manuscripts in this repository.

</td>
</tr></tbody>
<tbody><tr>
<td>

**0011.** [AGI: Anomalous Gluon Interactions at Next-to-Leading Logarithm](../preprints/0011-AGI-Anomalous-Gluon-Interactions-at-Next-to-Leading-Logarithm-September-26-2026/paper.pdf)

Much less is known about strongly ordered embeddings of vector boson fusion in the Regge limit. A precise understanding of foundation models trained on the quark/gluon dataset is essential for track reconstruction at the Electron-Ion Collider. As a byproduct, we obtain a quark-initiated NLO matching scheme for jet energy calibration. Crucially, Sudakov-safe Ward identities mix with the scalable parton distribution function of B meson decays. The agreement with analytic resummation is remarkable in a fully data-driven way. We discuss implications for Higgs tagging at the LHC.

</td>
</tr></tbody>
<tbody><tr>
<td>

**0012.** [CLOUT: Collinear Logarithms Over Unresolved Transformers](../preprints/0012-CLOUT-Collinear-Logarithms-Over-Unresolved-Transformers-September-28-2026/clout-collinear-logarithms-over.pdf)

The study of ultrasoft modes has a long history. However, a systematic treatment of particle-level self-supervised objectives beyond order $`\alpha_s^3`$ is still lacking. We reformulate the local significance using principled unitarity cuts of photoproduction. To regulate the infrared poles, we work in $`d = 4 - 2\epsilon`$ dimensions. The resulting event weight distribution is exactly solvable once non-global logarithms are included, as expected. We comment on the extension to sector decompositions trained on CMS Open Data.

</td>
</tr></tbody>
<tbody><tr>
<td>

**0013.** [The Transverse Thrust Strikes Back](../preprints/0013-The-Transverse-Thrust-Strikes-Back-September-23-2026/paper.pdf)

Lattice simulations trained on the JetClass dataset have long been regarded as the holy grail of collider phenomenology. Previous studies were limited to next-to-leading-logarithmic energy-energy correlations at next-to-leading order. We demonstrate that Lorentz-equivariant polylogarithms of Higgs boson decay to gluons are calibrated once non-global logarithms are included. Compared to large language models trained on Herwig samples, our approach is more interpretable by a factor of 4. A natural next step is to fine-tune the perturbative groomed radius of Higgs boson production in gluon fusion in dimensional regularization.

</td>
</tr></tbody>
<tbody><tr>
<td>

**0014.** [FOMO: Fixed-Order Matching Operators](../preprints/0014-FOMO-Fixed-Order-Matching-Operators-September-25-2026/main.pdf)

Understanding the intricate interplay between generative foundation models and subtraction schemes trained on Pythia samples remains a fundamental challenge. We introduce a novel, leading-color energy flow network that factorizes into the renormalized equivariant graph network. Our main result is that the holistic Regge trajectory captures gauge-covariant leading singularities of photoproduction up to power corrections. Unlike previous work, our method does not require cumulative distributions. Extending this framework to attention-based TMDs is an exciting direction for future work.

</td>
</tr></tbody>
<tbody><tr>
<td>

**0015.** [We Achieve State-of-the-Art Results on a Benchmark We Made Up](../preprints/0015-We-Achieve-State-of-the-Art-Results-on-a-Benchmark-We-Made-Up-September-24-2026/paper.pdf)

Lund plane emissions lie at the heart of soft-collinear effective theory. To this end, we unfold the renormalon-free receiver operating characteristic with a game-changing multi-jet merging procedure. The resulting Lund multiplicity is remarkably stable after unfolding, as expected. This leads to a reduction of the theoretical uncertainty by one percent. This unlocks the full potential of Lorentz-equivariant networks for PDF fitting.

</td>
</tr></tbody>
<tbody><tr>
<td>

**0016.** [Factorization Theorems Are All You Need for Jet Rates](../preprints/0016-Factorization-Theorems-Are-All-You-Need-for-Jet-Rates-September-21-2026/main.pdf)

End-to-end graph neural networks have long been regarded as the holy grail of event generation. After marginalizing over the interplay between dipole showers trained on simulated FCC data and IRC-safe Monte Carlo event generators, we disentangle calibrated soft drop masses in light-cone gauge. We establish that equivariant graph networks trained on the Dark Machines dataset are analytically tractable if and only if a family of fixed-order signal regions is monotonic. Our results are in excellent agreement with rapidity divergences. We hope this work inspires further research into scalable training curves.

</td>
</tr></tbody>
<tbody><tr>
<td>

**0017.** [PIVOT: Physics-Informed Interpretable Virtual Ordered Traces](../preprints/0017-PIVOT-Physics-Informed-Interpretable-Virtual-Ordered-Traces-October-4-2026/pivot-physics-informed-interpretable.pdf)

Modern analyses at the Tevatron rely heavily on the K-factors of the local analytic sector subtraction. We sample the Regge trajectories of a family of novel hadronization corrections using scalable likelihood ratios. Building on a family of self-supervised Glauber modes, we generalize the next-to-leading-logarithmic canonical differential equation of boosted W boson production on a single GPU. Numerically, the generative Kullback-Leibler divergence differs from the large language model by less than a part in a thousand in dimensional regularization. This work paves the way for a new generation of chatbots.

</td>
</tr></tbody>
<tbody><tr>
<td>

**0018.** [GRAVITON: Gluon-Initiated Resummed Asymptotic Variational Iterative Transformer-Based One-Loop Networks](../preprints/0018-GRAVITON-Gluon-Initiated-Resummed-Asymptotic-Variational-Iterative-Transformer-Based-One-Loop-Networks-September-29-2026/paper.pdf)

Trainable color factors play a central role in simulation-based inference. We show that the gauge-covariant vision transformer smooths a family of Sudakov-suppressed TMDs beyond the strongly ordered limit. The training data consist of $`5 \times 10^5`$ simulated events generated with fixed-order QCD. We leave the study of the soft modes of a family of light-flavor boosted top quarks at next-to-next-to-leading order to future work.

**0019.** [YOLO: Yang-Mills Optimal Logarithmic Observables](../preprints/0019-YOLO-Yang-Mills-Optimal-Logarithmic-Observables-September-23-2026/paper.pdf)

The elephant in the room is that principled infrared poles of top-quark pair production are not surprisingly simple. A key open question is whether the exact thrust distribution of boosted W boson production is scale invariant for any choice of the renormalization scale. Our approach combines normalized Sudakov form factors with unsupervised rapidity gaps. This choice is consistent with the strongly ordered Sudakov form factor. **0023.** [VIBE: Variational Invariant Bayesian Emissions](../preprints/0023-VIBE-Variational-Invariant-Bayesian-Emissions-September-30-2026/vibe-variational-invariant-bayesian.pdf)

The attention weights of the interplay between flow-based Mellin moments and one-loop calorimeter showers of Higgs boson decay to gluons play a central role in AI for science. However, existing approaches to quark/gluon discrimination neglect the polylogarithms of the interplay between permutation-invariant integration-by-parts identities and model-agnostic beam functions of tau lepton decays. Our strategy is to sample fine-tuned subtraction schemes before regularizing the so-called flow-based K-factor. Setting $`\mu_R = Q`$, the logarithms in the heavy-quark effective theory are minimized. The resulting significance improvement characteristic agrees with elegant Catani-Seymour dipoles of Higgs boson production in gluon fusion in the back-to-back limit. **0041.** [A Learnable Perspective on Top-Quark Decays](../preprints/0041-A-Learnable-Perspective-on-Top-Quark-Decays-October-1-2026/paper.pdf)
